* Fix a logical error (server abort in debug/sanitizer builds) when a multi-command `ALTER` such as `UPDATE ..., DELETE ...` was issued on an Iceberg table. Such mutations are now rejected with a clear `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` error. [#110347](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110347) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a parser bug where COMMENT was incorrectly consumed as an implicit alias when a SELECT query ended directly after a bare table identifier (e.g. `... FROM t COMMENT 'x'`), causing a misleading syntax error instead of the comment being applied to the view/table. [#110372](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110372) ([Aditya Kumar](https://github.com/adityaksolves)).
* Fixed a server crash (native stack overflow) when a deeply nested `Array`/`Tuple`/`Map`/`Object` literal is copied or destroyed, for example a query with a very deeply nested literal at a raised `max_parser_depth`. [#110393](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110393) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Rejects `vector_search_index_fetch_multiplier` values below 1.0 to prevent empty ANN search results when the computed fetch count truncates to zero. [#110452](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110452) ([Tamish Mhatre](https://github.com/tamish560)).
* Skips vector search optimization when `LIMIT ... WITH TIES` is used, because the optimization bounds the ANN search to exactly n candidates and rows tied with the n-th row are dropped. [#110453](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110453) ([Tamish Mhatre](https://github.com/tamish560)).
* Fix the logical error `Expected CommonSubplanReferenceStep to reference CommonSubplanStep`, the error `Subplan cannot be used to build pipeline`, and the logical error `Trying to extract chunk from ChunkBuffer before all inputs are finished` for `IN (subquery)` where the subquery contains a correlated subquery and the set is built during index analysis. [#110491](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110491) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix substitution of query parameters inside the definitions of a named `WINDOW` clause. Previously, parameters there were silently kept unsubstituted, and an unset `Identifier` parameter could lead to the exception `Logical error: '!part.empty()'` during `EXPLAIN SYNTAX`. [#110506](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110506) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed server startup failure for `MergeTree` tables on object storage disks when a leftover `txn_version.txt.tmp` file has broken disk-level metadata. [#110519](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110519) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a logical error in the filesystem cache (`Expected file ... not to exist`) that could occur when a background cache download failed with a non-`ErrnoException` (for example while `OPTIMIZE`-ing an Iceberg table), leaving an empty orphan cache file behind. [#110549](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110549) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading a corrupted `Native`-format stream whose `Dynamic` type count or `JSON`/`Object` path count is close to `SIZE_MAX`: such malformed input is now rejected with a clear `INCORRECT_DATA` error instead of an uncaught `std::length_error`. [#110590](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110590) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `ilike` operator for the defined text index expression. [#110595](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110595) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Fixed a server crash in the `MaterializedPostgreSQL` database engine that could happen when a table was detached (or its structure changed) while it was still queued for synchronization. [#110596](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110596) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `SET param_<name>` in the client (and `--param_<name>` flags) misbehaving when the parameter name matches a builtin setting name such as `limit`, `offset`, `max_threads`, or `log_comment`. Depending on the colliding setting the query failed with `CANNOT_PARSE_QUOTED_STRING`, the value was silently normalized (e.g. `max_threads=0` became `auto(N)`), or a setting alias name was lost. Such a parameter also broke every subsequent `SET param_*` in the same session. [#110597](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110597) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix reading subcolumns of a column that has a `DEFAULT` expression and is not materialized in a part (e.g. after `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` or during `ALTER TABLE MATERIALIZE COLUMN`): the subcolumn was filled with type default values instead of the evaluated `DEFAULT` expression, and the transposed vector distance functions (`cosineDistanceTransposed` and others) on such `QBit` columns failed with the `SIZES_OF_ARRAYS_DONT_MATCH` error. This closes [#110634](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/110634). [#110636](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110636) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` "Invalid partition key size: 0" that could abort an `INSERT` into a partitioned `MergeTree` table with `non_replicated_deduplication_window` enabled, when an async insert batch was only partially deduplicated (some `insert_deduplication_token`s were duplicates and some were new). [#110651](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110651) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A background asynchronous insert flush now stops promptly when killed with `KILL QUERY`; previously a flush of a large buffered payload kept parsing to the end and ignored the cancellation. [#110652](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110652) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Fixed wrong results from `GROUP BY ... optimize_aggregation_in_order` and `DISTINCT ... optimize_distinct_in_order` placed over a `partial_merge` JOIN. The read-in-order optimization was propagated through `PartialMergeJoin`, which re-sorts its left input by the join key and therefore does not preserve the left stream's original order, so rows were grouped incorrectly. [#110671](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110671) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a signed integer overflow in `toStartOfInterval` with an extreme `MONTH`, `WEEK`, or `DAY` interval count on `Date`/`Date32`/`DateTime64` values. [#110688](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110688) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` error for queries with a subquery in `FROM` when parallel replicas run in `custom_key_sampling` / `custom_key_range` mode. Such queries (e.g. `SELECT * FROM (SELECT id, k, v FROM t WHERE id < 20) ORDER BY k`) previously failed with `Not found column __table2.id in block ...`. [#110690](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110690) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `readWKT` rejecting WKT strings with leading whitespace (e.g. `readWKT(' POINT(1 2)')`), which are accepted by the typed `readWKTPoint`/`readWKTPolygon`/... readers and by the WKT grammar. [#110706](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110706) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a logical error (`Column ... already added for reading`, a server abort in debug/sanitizer builds) when the same text-index predicate is used in both `PREWHERE` and `WHERE` of a query over a `Merge` table on top of a `Distributed` table. [#110710](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110710) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` error for the transposed distance functions over `QBit` columns (such as `cosineDistanceTransposed`) called with a scalar-subquery or constant reference vector when parallel replicas are enabled. This closes [#110719](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/110719). [#110729](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110729) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a rare `LOGICAL_ERROR` ("Unexpected number of parts to remove from parts_queue") that could abort the server on a `ReplicatedMergeTree` when two partition operations (for example two `MOVE PARTITION` / `REPLACE PARTITION` queries, or one racing a background `DROP_RANGE`) cancelled the background part check over overlapping ranges at the same time. [#110738](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110738) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `Hash is not set for serialization` logical error that could occur when a column with a `Quantized(...)` codec was nested inside a poolable serialization, for example a `Merge` table over sources whose same-named column has different types (merged into a `Variant`). [#110776](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110776) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server hang when multiplying a `median`/`quantile` (ReservoirSampler-backed) aggregate function state by a huge integer constant (e.g. `medianState(x) * 18446744073709551615`). The query became unresponsive to cancellation and could run indefinitely. [#110779](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110779) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server crash (integer division by zero) when a quota with a zero-length interval (for example CREATE QUOTA q FOR INTERVAL 0 SECOND MAX queries = 1000) was consumed. A non-positive quota interval duration is now rejected at quota creation. [#110846](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110846) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Fixed `throwIf(notLike(col, pattern))` (and other `throwIf` over a function) throwing unconditionally when `col` is `LowCardinality`. The default `LowCardinality` implementation ran `throwIf` on the whole dictionary, which always holds the reserved default value even when no row references it, so a non-zero value in that unused slot made `throwIf` throw for data that does not satisfy the condition. [#110864](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110864) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Propagate a query's active roles (SET ROLE / role=) to remote nodes on parallel-replica and Distributed reads over an interserver-secret cluster; previously they fell back to the user's default roles, evaluating row policies inconsistently with the initiator. [#110867](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110867) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fixed Iceberg tables producing duplicate field IDs after `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` when the initial schema contains nested fields (`Tuple`/`Array`/`Map`). `last-column-id` now records the maximum assigned field ID including nested children, so a subsequently added column no longer reuses a nested field ID and reads no longer fail with `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION` (`Duplicate field id`). [#110884](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110884) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash in FIPS builds when using an Ed25519 SSH key (`CREATE USER ... IDENTIFIED WITH ssh_key ... TYPE 'ssh-ed25519'`, or such a key in `users.xml`). Ed25519 is not FIPS-approved; such keys are now rejected with a clear `LIBSSH_ERROR` error. [#110891](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110891) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* The transposed distance functions over `QBit` (`cosineDistanceTransposed`, `L2DistanceTransposed`, `dotProductTransposed`) now use a bounded low-precision reconstruction of a value truncated to `precision` bit planes, instead of reconstructing it to the coarse cell's lower edge (dropped bits zero-filled). Zero-filling biased every reconstructed value towards zero and was degenerate at low precision: for `QBit(BFloat16)` at precision 1 only the sign bit survives, so every value reconstructed to `±0.0` and the distance was the same constant for every row, carrying no ranking information. The reconstruction now depends on which bits are dropped: raw `Int8` codes are reconstructed to their cell centre (matching the `Lloyd-Max` reconstruction the `...TransposedQuantized` path already used); a floating-point value at precision 1 becomes a proper sign quantization; a floating-point value that keeps its whole exponent and drops only mantissa bits is reconstructed to the bounded midpoint within its own binade (while a genuine zero or `±0.0` is left at exact zero and the non-finite cell is carved out so `±inf` stays exactly infinite; see the note below on the ambiguous `NaN` cell); and while exponent bits are still being truncated the bounded lower edge is kept, so a smaller `precision` trades accuracy for speed without blowing a magnitude up by orders of magnitude. Full-precision results are unchanged. This closes [#110898](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/110898). [#110911](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110911) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a use-after-free segfault during graceful server shutdown after Iceberg tables were queried from sessions that also used temporary tables. [#110914](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110914) ([Miсhael Stetsyuk](https://github.com/mstetsyuk)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR: Cannot sum Bools` (server abort in debug/sanitizer builds) when merging `sumMap`/`sumMapWithOverflow`/`sumMapFiltered` aggregate states with `Bool` values that were serialized in the old (version 0) state format. The same fix also corrects two silent wrong-result cases with version-0 `Bool` states: map keys of type `Bool` were not deduplicated across states, and zero-value compaction dropped the wrong entries. [#110922](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110922) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash when an aggregate function with the `-Tuple` combinator was used with `RESPECT NULLS` and produced an intermediate state (`-State`, distributed aggregation, `WITH ROLLUP`/`WITH CUBE`). The combined function was named after the wrong state variant, so its serialized `AggregateFunction` type re-resolved to an incompatible in-memory layout on a round-trip. [#110930](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110930) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `PREWHERE` (and row-level policies) on an Iceberg column that was renamed by schema evolution silently returning `0` rows for old data files. Such queries now return the correct rows, matching `WHERE`. [#110941](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110941) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`LazyMaterializingTransform: Number of rows in lazy chunk N does not match number of offsets M`) that could happen for vector search queries with `vector_search_with_rescoring = 1` combined with lazy materialization when several distances tie. [#111003](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111003) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a LOGICAL_ERROR ("No set is registered for key") in ALTER TABLE ... DROP COLUMN, ALTER TABLE ... DELETE/UPDATE mutations, and lightweight DELETE, on tables that have an ALIAS column whose expression uses an IN operator (including through another ALIAS column). [#111039](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111039) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Fixed silent data loss with async inserts and deduplication (`async_insert=1`, `async_insert_deduplicate=1`). When several async-insert entries with distinct `insert_deduplication_token` values were coalesced into one flush that wrote to disjoint partitions, each token was registered in the deduplication log of every partition the flush touched, not only the partition its own rows landed in. A later insert reusing one of those tokens in a partition it never wrote to was then silently deduplicated away. Tokens are now registered only against the partition their rows actually landed in. [#111049](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111049) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading `Arrow`/`ArrowStream` data with empty nested `Array`/`Map` columns produced by Apache Arrow Java < 19.0.0 (bundled with Apache Spark), which were previously rejected with an `INCORRECT_DATA` error about the offsets buffer being too small. [#111101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111101) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* This patch fixes stale reads on plain-rewritable disks with the page cache enabled. [#111105](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111105) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Fixed asynchronous Native-format inserts so one buffered entry that becomes incompatible after `ALTER ... MODIFY COLUMN` no longer causes the whole batch to fail. [#111108](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111108) ([Mikhail f. Shiryaev](https://github.com/Felixoid)).
* Fix `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` error for constants of `Variant` type (including `Geometry`) in distributed queries and queries with parallel replicas. [#111136](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111136) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).

#### Build/Testing/Packaging Improvement
* Build `musl` from sources instead of using vendored binaries. [#97452](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/97452) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Not for changelog (test improvement). [#103987](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/103987) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Randomize query_plan_optimize_join_order_randomize in stateless tests. [#105995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/105995) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Use SIMD `memcmp`/`memcpy`/`memmove`/`memset`/`bcmp`/`memmem` implementations from LLVM-libc. [#107566](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107566) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Added a regression test for inserting into a table referenced by a materialized view whose query joins a column-renaming subquery using `USING`, under both analyzers. [#108079](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108079) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The performance comparison test framework now accepts shell-script tests (`<query type="shell">`) in addition to SQL queries, for end-to-end measurements such as HTTP latency, response compression, and tool startup time. [#108081](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108081) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Randomize the default compression codec (`LZ4`, `ZSTD(1)`, `ZSTD(3)`) in stateless tests. [#108093](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108093) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Remove usages of `curl` library across codebase and contribs. [#108296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108296) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Updated `mariadb-connector-c` to 3.4.9. [#108328](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108328) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Update `libssh` to 0.12.0. [#108329](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108329) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fix transaction commit hang on macOS (atomic wait without notify). [#108375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108375) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Update `abseil-cpp` to LTS `20260526.0`. [#108991](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108991) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Added a `COMPRESS_DEBUG_SECTIONS` CMake option that compresses DWARF debug sections in object files (`-gz`), auto-enabled where the compiler supports it, to shrink Debug build directories; final binaries are left uncompressed, so runtime stack-trace symbolization is unaffected. [#109306](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109306) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fix compression codec tests non-standard constant compile issue on macOS. [#109443](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109443) ([Yue Ni](https://github.com/niyue)).
* Debug builds now compile the vendored Rust crates with reduced debug info (`debug = 1`, cargo's `limited` level) and no incremental cache (`REDUCE_RUST_DEBUG_INFO`, on by default), shrinking Rust build artifacts (~12 → ~7 GB from scratch) and preventing unbounded cargo cache growth across rebuilds. Set `-DREDUCE_RUST_DEBUG_INFO=OFF` to restore full DWARF and incremental compilation. [#109471](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109471) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Built the bundled LLVM toolchain with zlib so the shipped clang accepts `-gz=zlib` (compressed DWARF debug sections), and added the missing `clang++-21` symlink so the toolchain's C and C++ compilers are the same install. [#109596](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109596) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Support the streaming distributed query exchange and STREAM reads on macOS. [#109769](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109769) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Not for changelog (test-only change). [#109830](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109830) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* Use PGO+BOLT optimized clang toolchain for CI builds. [#109864](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109864) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Bump libpng from 1.6.48 to 1.6.58. [#109869](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109869) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Bump libarchive from 3.8.7 to 3.8.8. [#109874](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109874) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Bump c-ares from 1.34.6 to 1.34.8. [#109905](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109905) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Fix krb5 CVE-2026-40355 & CVE-2026-40356. [#109910](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109910) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Removed the `libpng` third-party dependency. `FORMAT PNG` now uses a small built-in PNG encoder (backed by the bundled zlib) instead. [#110051](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110051) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Added tests covering the full matrix of comparisons and arithmetic over mixed type pairs (`Decimal` vs integers and date/time types, and pairs involving `Int128`/`UInt128`/`Int256`/`UInt256`), including result types, all combinations of constant and non-constant arguments, and exceptional cases. [#110135](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110135) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Track raw C allocations (not going through `operator new`) in the memory tracker on macOS by wrapping the jemalloc malloc zone, so memory limits are enforced for them. [#110370](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110370) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Added regression tests for reading PostgreSQL `boolean[]` arrays through the `PostgreSQL` and `MaterializedPostgreSQL` engines. [#110569](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110569) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add integration tests that previously only existed in the ClickHouse Cloud-only test suite: `test_drop_data` (`max_table_size_to_drop`/`max_partition_size_to_drop` guards), `test_settings_constraints_config_profiles` (settings-profile constraint enforcement under a cloud-shaped config), `test_create_dictionary_in_startup_script` (dictionary creation via `startup_scripts`), `test_storage_delta_shuffles` (`DeltaLake` reads under `make_distributed_plan`), and AWS STS assume-role coverage added to the existing `test_storage_delta` and `test_storage_s3_queue` suites (`extra_credentials(role_arn = ..., role_session_name = ...)`). `test_storage_s3_queue`'s new test needed a small, generic extension to the `create_table()` helper in `helpers/s3_queue_common.py` (pass-through `extra_credentials` clause, schema-less `CREATE TABLE` support) — ported verbatim from the private fork's helper. [#110598](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110598) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix silent `memmove` corruption in `-O0` builds (`-DDEBUG_O_LEVEL=0`) on machines with AVX-512, which crashed `llvm-tblgen` during the build. [#110904](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110904) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).

* Add unit tests and documentation for the `force_add_bucket` DataLake catalog setting introduced by [#104120](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/104120). [#106655](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/106655) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Mark caught LOGICAL_ERROR exception as logged in BackupDataFileNameGenerator gtest. [#106668](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/106668) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `timeSeries*ToGrid()` aggregation functions: - were optimized and simplified; - adding new functions is now much easier; - buckets are now aligned to both step and window (that will allow to store just aggregation data and not all the samples for functions like `first_over_time`, `max_over_time`); - final aggregation is now much faster when `window>>step` because we don't recompute it from scratch for every grid point anymore and we use two-stack approach instead; - the format version was bumped because the layout of buckets changed (these `timeSeries*` aggregation functions are experimental so not having migration code seems ok). [#106724](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/106724) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).

* Move KeyCondition relaxedness to RPN atoms. [#107462](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107462) ([Michael Jarrett](https://github.com/EmeraldShift)).
* Small bugfixes. - Add a few more paths for the Alias engine in BuzzHouse. - Add more ALTER queries from Spark. - Fuzz more `* something` expressions from AST fuzzer. - More settings. [#107536](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107536) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Add Praktika-based CreateRelease workflow and move create_release.py to ci/jobs. [#107578](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107578) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Randomize SMT fetch-part-in-memory settings in tests. [#107708](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107708) ([Den Kalantaevskii](https://github.com/Diskein)).
* Add sccache-warmup release builds to MasterCI. [#107766](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107766) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Not required. [#107794](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107794) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Stabilize llvm coverage: Part 3. [#107861](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107861) ([Alexey Bakharew](https://github.com/alexbakharew)).
* Fix flaky `test_checking_s3_blobs_paranoid` by retrying the `broken_s3` mock control channel. [#107895](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107895) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* This patch fixes the list requests implementation, where each list feature was implemented as an additional ZooKeeper request type. In the previous approach, TestKeeper was forced to reuse ZooKeeperImpl implementation, which leads to invalid request construction in multiple places: - https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107849 - https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107842. [#107969](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107969) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).

* This patch fixes a possible race condition in the intersecting parts produced by the move partition operation. The problem is that the commit of moved parts and block number allocation were not under the same parts lock. Because of that, parts were in the PreActive state with an already allocated block number but without a lock, which means intersecting merge can be assigned, since, for a regular merge tree, we do not check for committed blocks during merge selection. [#108922](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108922) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).

* Extend BuzzHouse, AST fuzzer and La Casa Del Dolor to cover more SQL surface. - BuzzHouse: fuzz hypothetical indexes, Int8 QBit, MODIFY CONSTRAINT, the sparse_grams index and text-index parameters, and more CREATE TABLE options; load input/output formats from system.formats instead of a hardcoded list for easier maintenance. - AST fuzzer: fuzz wider integer and floating-point corner cases, and swap more groups of related functions. Also fuzz GROUP BY ALL/LIMIT BY ALL, CROSS/COMMA joins, SAMPLE ... OFFSET, named WINDOW definitions, WITH FILL ... STALENESS, Bool and Array/Tuple/Map literals, stacked aggregate combinators and parametric-aggregate parameters, and ALTER ... MODIFY command mutation. - Extend casa_del_dolor, the Spark/data-lake configuration fuzzer. Fuzz more server, Keeper and filesystem-cache settings, and generate harder data (nasty strings, integer/float/decimal extremes, non-finite values in Variant JSON). Widen Iceberg/Delta/Paimon table coverage: more table properties, partition transforms, branch/tag DDL and maintenance procedures (fast_forward, rollback_to_snapshot/_timestamp, Paimon rollback/expire_partitions). Prune incompatible option combinations so generated CREATE TABLEs are valid (Paimon first-row merge engine, deletion vectors, snapshot.num-retained min/max; Delta deletion vectors vs incremental symlink manifests; VARIANT forces Parquet; drop unavailable brotli/lzo codecs). Harden the Spark session lifecycle: disk-backed scratch space with a stale-directory sweeper, register tables only after a successful CREATE, and serialize session use. [#109194](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109194) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).

* Fix the AST fuzzer silently burning through its query corpus and exiting early with success, and make the targeted AST fuzzer use its whole time budget by restarting over the corpus. [#109725](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109725) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).

* CI: add nightly workflow to regenerate reference documentation from source. [#109746](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109746) ([Shaun Struwig](https://github.com/Blargian)).
* Move AST-fuzzer max_threads cap into the shared limit-recursion-settings.xml. [#109755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109755) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Set `CH Inc sync` in `Code Review` and unblock on tidy build. [#109760](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109760) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* This patch fixes abstraction leaks introduced in https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/95079. [#109766](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109766) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Move tests/ci/test_* unit tests into ci/tests/ so they run in CI. [#109777](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109777) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Add regression test for clear error on UPDATE of non-existent column. [#109793](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109793) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Classify `external_id` as an S3 auth field in dynamic disk definitions. [#109795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109795) ([Pablo Marcos](https://github.com/pamarcos)).
* tests: make 00956_sensitive_data_masking faster by removing introspection in case of failures. [#109834](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109834) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Bound each system-table dump with a timeout in Collect logs. [#109837](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109837) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).

* Fix in unreleased change: `BACKUP`/`RESTORE ... ON CLUSTER` failed with `ACCESS_DENIED` when the initiating user was allowed to use server-managed S3 credentials (`s3_allow_server_credentials_in_user_queries = 1` in its profile) but the default profile pinned the setting with a readonly constraint. The initiator's value is now propagated to the other hosts through the distributed DDL entry instead of being re-clamped there. [#109903](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109903) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).

[#110655](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110655) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Pin PR build tweak and restore new/cloud version types in extracted CHVersion. [#110661](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110661) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Fix a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Current component is empty, please set it for your scope using Coordination::setCurrentComponent`) that could abort the server in debug/sanitizer builds (or fail under `enforce_keeper_component_tracking=1`) when a zero-copy exclusive lock was released outside any Keeper component scope. This happened in two sibling paths: (1) when a replicated merge or mutation task holding the lock was destroyed before its explicit unlock was reached, e.g. on cancellation, and (2) when a background zero-copy part move acquired/released the lock. `MutateFromLogEntryTask` and `MergeFromLogEntryTask` now release their `zero_copy_lock` under a `Coordination::setCurrentComponent` guard in an explicit destructor (matching the existing `DDLWorker::shutdown` precedent for `EphemeralNodeHolder`), and `MergeTreeData::moveParts` sets the component around its zero-copy section (`tryCreateZeroCopyExclusiveLock`, `waitZeroCopyLockToDisappear`, and the local lock's release). A deterministic regression test (`04603_zero_copy_lock_dtor_keeper_component`, adapted from [#110670](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/110670)) covers both paths: two pauseable failpoints let it destroy a mutation task while it provably holds the zero-copy lock, and a synchronous `ALTER TABLE ... MOVE PART` to a zero-copy disk exercises the `moveParts` path. [#110664](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110664) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Use in-region AWS Ubuntu apt mirror in docker builds. [#110669](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110669) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* Guard flaky test for long sanitized runnings. [#110679](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110679) ([Mikhail f. Shiryaev](https://github.com/Felixoid)).
* Change conditional uncompressed binary upload to unconditional compressed binary for fast tests. [#110689](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110689) ([Mikhail f. Shiryaev](https://github.com/Felixoid)).
* Web UI: fix the last two stale new-tab gesture comments in play.html. [#110705](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110705) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix CI errors like this one https://s3.amazonaws.com/clickhouse-test-reports/json.html?REF=master&sha=ba01db87ae3860cffa1b0552a7751e566ed14e75&name_0=MasterCI. [#110712](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110712) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* `FORGET PARTITION` is not a query that can be fuzzed. [#110720](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110720) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Bump runner-init version to 74. [#110731](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110731) ([Alexei Fedotov](https://github.com/leshikus)).
* Not for changelog. [#110734](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110734) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Not required. [#110742](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110742) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky timeout in 03209_json_type_horizontal_merges. [#110755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110755) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Sync critical files from the private repository. [#110756](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110756) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Make `04498_query_condition_cache_local_files` non-parallel. [#110763](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110763) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Retry gh auth token validation on transient GitHub API errors. [#110769](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110769) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add regression test for implicit-boolean WHERE filter on projection and parallel-replicas read paths. [#110797](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110797) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* This pr adds adaptive scaling to the `BlobKiller` infrastructure. [#110817](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110817) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* ### Description. [#110841](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110841) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test 02421_simple_queries_for_opentelemetry. [#110849](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110849) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add regression test for BAD_TYPE_OF_FIELD in Parquet pushdown over Dynamic/JSON/Variant columns. [#110890](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110890) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky test 01017_uniqCombined_memory_usage. [#110894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110894) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky 03008_deduplication_insert_into_partitioned_table. [#110895](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110895) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `randomHadamardTransform` now transforms vectors whose length is `2^N * 9` (for example `1152 = 128 * 9`, common in some embedding models) exactly, without zero-padding to the next power of two, so the output keeps the input dimension. [#110910](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110910) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Not applicable (CI-only change). [#110912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110912) ([Shaun Struwig](https://github.com/Blargian)).
* Fix short read of product-quantization codebook on wide parts. [#110926](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110926) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `04603_quota_zero_interval` breaking parallel and stress runs. [#110954](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110954) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Add regression test for correlated subquery with `GROUPING SETS` and `group_by_use_nulls`. [#110956](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110956) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix flaky 03921_kafka_formats (tolerate Kafka at-least-once re-delivery). [#110989](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110989) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The CI flaky check maps a changed auxiliary file back to the tests that own it (`_tests_owning_data_file` in `ci/jobs/scripts/find_tests.py`): candidates are narrowed to tests sharing the fixture's five-digit prefix, and among those it prefers the ones whose body references the fixture's literal filename, falling back to *all* prefix siblings otherwise. [#110991](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110991) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Docs: regenerate reference documentation from source. [#111013](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111013) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* Add PromQL function `increase`. [#111023](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111023) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).
* Remove `test_library_bridge`. [#111024](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111024) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* CI: size stateless per-test memory limit by sanitizer build, not `asan_ubsan` substring. [#111028](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111028) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Report the crashed server log on multi-server stateless runs. [#111065](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111065) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* CI: rename report messages status. [#111079](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111079) ([Max Kainov](https://github.com/maxknv)).
* ReaderExecutor: coordinate cleanup, run-anchored reach, serve-to-bound long connections. [#111088](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111088) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixes behavior in the unreleased [#106855](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/106855), which has not shipped in a stable release. [#111120](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111120) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix flaky test_rename_distributed_parallel_insert_and_select. [#111121](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111121) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Add test that Remote/RemoteSecure storage engines respect remote_url_allow_hosts. [#111140](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111140) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix flaky 03630_benchmark_accept_invalid_certificate (SOCKET_TIMEOUT). [#111168](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111168) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Sync Private. [#111187](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111187) ([Mikhail Artemenko](https://github.com/Michicosun)).
* Docs: regenerate reference documentation from source. [#111190](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111190) ([clickhouse-gh[bot]](https://github.com/apps/clickhouse-gh)).
* The difference between stripped and unstripped binaries is 87MB vs 691MB. On OK fast tests we will upload stripped binaries. [#111224](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111224) ([Mikhail f. Shiryaev](https://github.com/Felixoid)).
* Add a nightly CI job preparing the changelog for the upcoming release. [#111255](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111255) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
